Title: Die Hutterer: Österreichische Wiedertäufer und Kommunisten in Amerika
Creator: Rudolf Wolkan (title page, garbled Fraktur OCR, line 20; host record)
Publisher: Wien: Wiener Bibliophilen-Gesellschaft, 1918 (title page)
Publication Date: 1918 (title page, read directly; preface signed Wien, September 1918, OCR lines 124-126)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1918); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: deu
Source: archive.org item diehuttererste00wolk, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-11 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (429,990) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1918, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair to poor: the Fraktur is systematically misread, so quote only after checking the page images. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Rudolf Wolkan's history of the Hutterites to their colonies in South Dakota and Manitoba, dedicated to Elias Walter of Frankfort, South Dakota. A letter of John Horsch at about OCR line 59; the chapter on the Hutterites in America at about 3919; an appendix of Hutterite hymns, among them a song of Jakob Walter, 1800, Russia, at about 8853 and one made at Huttertal in South Russia in 1849 at about 8955.
